declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %r = call i32 @"example.com/stdtest/fmt.will$main"()
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %r
}
define internal { ptr, i64 } @"will.show.Array<u8>"(ptr %data) {
entry:
  %out = alloca { ptr, i64 }
  %d = load ptr, ptr %data
  %lenp = getelementptr i8, ptr %data, i64 8
  %n = load i64, ptr %lenp
  call void @willrt_show_str(ptr %d, i64 %n, ptr %out)
  %s = load { ptr, i64 }, ptr %out
  ret { ptr, i64 } %s
}
